Process budget: remove commas but keep currency symbol\n    if '예산' in data:\n        budget = data['예산']\n        # Match currency symbol + number with commas\n        match = re.match(r'^(\\D*)([\\d,]+)$', budget)\n        if match:\n            currency = match.group(1)\n            number = match.group(2).replace(',', '')\n            data['예산'] = currency + number\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef calculate_month_span(date_text):\n    \"\"\"Calculate inclusive month span from date range like '2026-08 ~ 2026-10'.\"\"\"\n    # Look for patterns like 2026-08 ~ 2026-10 or 2026.08 ~ 2026.10\n    pattern = r'(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})\\s*~\\s*(\\d{4})[\\.-](\\d{2})'\n    match = re.search(pattern, date_text)\n\n    if match:\n        start_year = int(match.group(1))\n        start_month = int(match.group(2))\n        end_year = int(match.group(3))\n        end_month = int(match.group(4))\n\n        # Calculate inclusive months: (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n        months = (end_year - start_year) * 12 + (end_month - start_month) + 1\n        return months\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef process_paragraph(p_elem, data):\n    \"\"\"Process a single paragraph element.\"\"\"\n    # Find all hp:t text elements within this paragraph\n    t_elements = p_elem.findall('.//hp:t', NAMESPACES)\n\n    if not t_elements:\n        return False\n\n    # Merge all text content\n    merged_text = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in t_elements)\n\n    # Check for placeholders\n    original_text = merged_text\n\n    # Replace all {{key}} patterns\n    for key, value in data.items():\n        placeholder = f'{{{{{key}}}}}'\n        if placeholder in merged_text:\n            merged_text = merged_text.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n    # Check if this is a phase line and add month span\n    phase_match = re.match(r'(\\d)단계:', merged_text)\n    if phase_match and ('~' in merged_text or '–' in merged_text):\n        months = calculate_month_span(merged_text)\n        if months and f'({months}개월)' not in merged_text:\n            merged_text = merged_text + f' ({months}개월)'\n\n    # Check if any changes were made\n    if merged_text == original_text:\n        return False\n\n    # Update the first hp:t element with full text\n    t_elements[0].text = merged_text\n\n    # Remove all other hp:t elements (they're now redundant)\n    # We need to find and clear them from their parent hp:run elements\n    for t_elem in t_elements[1:]:\n        parent = find_parent(p_elem, t_elem)\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(t_elem)\n\n    # Remove hp:lineSegArray elements to prevent layout issues\n    for lineseg in p_elem.findall('hp:linesegarray', NAMESPACES):\n        p_elem.remove(lineseg)\n\n    return True\n\n\ndef find_parent(root, target):\n    \"\"\"Find the parent element of target within root.\"\"\"\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        for child in elem:\n            if child is target:\n                return elem\n    return None\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path, data):\n    \"\"\"Process a single XML file.\"\"\"\n    # Parse XML while preserving the namespace\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    modified = False\n\n    # Process all paragraphs\n    for p_elem in root.findall('.//hp:p', NAMESPACES):\n        if process_paragraph(p_elem, data):\n            modified = True\n\n    # Verify no {{ or }} remain in text\n    for t_elem in root.iter('{http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML}t'):\n        if t_elem.text:\n            if '{{' in t_elem.text or '}}' in t_elem.text:\n                raise ValueError(f\"Unreplaced placeholder found: {t_elem.text}\")\n\n    # Write back preserving XML declaration\n    if modified:\n        tree.write(xml_path, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return modified\n\n\ndef create_hwpx(source_dir, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create HWPX file from processed directory.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(source_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, source_dir)\n                zf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    hwpx_extract_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work'\n    json_path = '/root/project_proposal.json'\n    output_hwpx = '/root/project_proposal_ready.hwpx'\n\n    # Load JSON data\n    data = load_json_data(json_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded data: {json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)}\")\n\n    #
